tbmm-2017-01-18sit04spe0377par0339	Mr. President, dear lawmakers, I salute you all. Turkey's Grand National Assembly is on the verge of a historic decision. We will vote on a proposal that will deeply affect the position of the House, the constitutional system, which does not resemble any of the constitutional changes before this. It's an offer that replaces national will management with one person, takes over the House's powers, shares the legislature, and eliminates control authority. Dear friends, we are going through the darkest and most uncertain days of our republic's history. Our citizens are under threat of terrorism. Bombs are going off in our cities, innocent citizens are dying. The news of the martyrs is breaking our hearts. Every day the stoves go out, the pain gets worse. Fire in the economy is burning our citizen's pockets, 6 million people are unemployed. Inflation, the loss of value in our money and the exponential increase. The people are looking for bread, tradesmen. By the president's word, water ran out in Tulumba. No jobs, no production. In such an environment, we're discussing the Constitutional proposal to change the regime. Dear lawmakers, our ancestors have created a homeland from nothing and nothing but the National Liberation War against seven heifers. In the light of the Republic, they have exalted our state with wisdom and faith. Ataturk, who took national will on the frontlines as the sole basis of the Liberation War, is the unconditional nation of the state of Ataturk, who declared the establishment of the Republic of Turkey as the basic principle of our great Parliament, because today, just like today, there was no one behind our Parliament, there was one or this; there was a nation, there was a nation, there was a national will. Today we have the Coven and the entire nation behind it. Our republic, which grew up on the principles of a democratic, social law state, has given our country, our nation, uncountable values. Thanks to that unwavering will, the poor sons of the people who came out of Anatolia took advantage of the republic's possibilities, and at this time, they had the will and honor of ruling this beautiful country. Our Republic history is filled with examples of poor folk children leaving their village, finishing college, having a say in the country's administration, and sitting in the seat of the founding fathers of the country. That's what makes it our republic, our republic's gains, our democracy. If the son of an officer from the village of Ballıca of Tunceli Nazımiye, today can be President of the Republican People's Party established by Ataturk (Applause from CHP), if the son of a coast captain from Rize South is President of the Republic of Turkey today, then this is the work of Topal Dursun, the son of Topal Dursun of Erzincan from Refahiye Kayı village of Erzincan. For this, our republic is virtuous and precious to us because the republic is an orphan. For him, we need to appreciate our republic well, understand it well, and protect it like our eyeballs, but unfortunately, this offer will eliminate national sovereignty, the superiority of the nation and the gains of our republic. Dear friends, while we oppose this arrangement, when we criticize it, we don't evaluate it through a name or a party. We're seeing how this regime change is going to darken our country's horizons, end our democracy. Today we are strong, we do whatever we want. Don't think about it. One day, if a person you've equipped with unlimited power and power but you don't support is elected President, and he makes us look for our days with wax, what will you do then? We made this country for your children, your grandchildren. We're responsible for this nightmare. Can you say that? Can you handle such a heavy plague? The day we lose our Republic's gains, it'll be too late and we'll be on a road that can't be turned back.

tbmm-2017-01-18sit05spe0656par0059	Mr. President, I have taken a pledge on the 6th amendment of Constitutional Amendment Proposal 447. Regarding this proposal, Mr. President said three days ago: "The adoption of this constitutional amendment will have a doping effect on our country. I thank Mr. President for that definition. No academic, no lawyer, no deputy has ever made such a sitting description since this proposal came into our lives. I think this amendment is literally a doping. It will have a doping effect on politics, as described in sports literature as moral and illegal. (Applause from CHP rows) Doping is banned in order to increase sporting performance and is defined as the use of foreign substances in the body or the use of abnormal amounts of any substance. This is the same thing as a constitutional amendment. This proposal, which is completely foreign to our country, which has nothing to do with our culture, is a one-maner, who disrupts our brotherhood, gives authority to a single person abnormally, and is prepared in extraordinary circumstances, and is imposed in extraordinary ways, is contrary to the Constitution, which puts grass in the bell of our heavenly homeland. Look, doping will cause palpitations, precious MPs. As we have seen from the economic, social, and political consequences that have occurred in our country since the day you brought this proposal to Parliament, doping causes hypertension, even death by heart attack. We don't need doping, we need normalization, precious MPs. The substance we're talking about is a self-destructive substance that turns Parliament into a live bomb. With this clause, you remove the one effective way of controlling the House. We've brought political responsibility to the president. You say, for God's sake, in the first place in the Commission, you've even prohibited this parliament from discussing the activities of the state. What's left of it? It's just a congressional investigation, and I'm afraid you've rendered it virtually ineffective. Because even today, it's impossible to find 400 lawmakers in a situation where you can't find 15 brave lawmakers to uphold the sovereignty of the nation, to hold their own authority, to oppose the pressures, and to open a parliamentary investigation, and you know that very well. Again, I'm looking at the recent explanations, which are said in Aristotle's logic: "The Republic is the choice of the people, and the people are choosing this offer; so the republic does not fall apart." With these conclusions at the elementary school level, I'm afraid you're destroying all the republic's savings. The Republic is only a republic with its qualities. Unfortunately, without the legal state, without the secular state, the democratic principles of state are not an empty republic. You know as well as I do that North Korea is a republic, Iran is a republic, Syria and Iraq. If the Republic is empty, it's not a republic. Now, you're also saying that the House has a system of Giotin, it's going to be mutual. The same system, the same dissolution, brought Israel to the joint election in 1996-2001, but unfortunately there were two government crises in four years, and they returned to the parliamentary regime. With these examples, dear lawmakers, please don't waste our children's time. Come on, dear friends, in historical circumstances, no one has ever been given this much authority in our history in which we share their mistakes, their goodness. We've had proverbs that have come from experience for centuries. What did our ancestors say? There's something wrong with one hand, there's a sound of two hands. If we want to be a strong country, if we want no one to stand in front of us, all we need is unity and unity, freedom and democracy. I'm sure there are some among you who resent this arrogance, this power poisoning. tbmm-2017-01-18sit06spe0794par0128	Congressmans, I read the result of a secret vote on the 6th Amendment to the Constitution of the Republic of Turkey. ▪ Number of votes used: 485 Members of parliament, the 6th article of the proposal has been accepted. Now, I'm going to read the 7th article of the proposal: MADDE 7-2709 has been changed in the following way, along with the 101st article of the Law. ▪ A. Candidate and selection The president of Madde 101-President is elected directly from among Turkish citizens, who are 40 years old, who have studied high school, who are qualified to be elected MP. The President's mandate is five years. One person can be elected President twice at most. Political party groups can nominate at least 100,000 voters to the presidency, with political parties alone or together with at least five per cent of the total valid vote in the latest general elections. The President-elected MP's membership in Turkey's Grand National Assembly ends. In the election with the general vote, the candidate President will be elected the only majority of the valid votes. If that majority cannot be reached in the first vote, the second vote will be held on the second Sunday following this vote. Two candidates who received the most votes in the first vote, and the candidate President, who received the majority of the valid votes, is elected. If one of the candidates who qualified for the second vote fails to participate in the election for any reason, the second vote is carried out on the basis of the first vote. If the second vote remains a single candidate, the vote will be held in the form of a referendum. The candidate will be elected President if the only majority of the valid votes is received. In the vote, only the election of the President will be renewed if the candidate fails to get the majority of the valid votes. If the elections fail, the current President's position will continue until the new one is in office. Other procedures and foundations for presidential elections are regulated by law. Members of parliament, there are seven motions on the 7th article, five of which are the same property. My first five motions are the same. You take national sovereignty in this way, you hand it over to one person's will, you coat that person with all the legal armor, you give all the authority to that person, and you tell that person that you will represent the unity and integrity of the nation, you will be independent, you will be neutral, you will appoint the judiciary, but you will be party and you will be party leader at the same time. Dear friends, you should know that your loyalty is not to one person, it is not to one understanding; your devotion is to the nation, your loyalty is to the Parliament. You will have to use the authority you have received from the people in the House. The people didn't give you this authority so that you could entrust it to one member of the House.
